### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. |---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C7h > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C7h | {sigma_h[10]} |
| C7h > C7 | yes | yes | yes | C7h | {C7^1[1]} |

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

| chain | ordinary | projective | both | abelian from | subgroups |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C10h > C10 | yes | yes | yes | C10h | {C10^1[1]} |
| C10h > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C10h | {sigma_h[15]} |
| C10h > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C10h | {C2[5]} |
| C10h > C2h | yes | yes | yes | C10h | {C2[5], sigma_h[15]} |
| C10h > C5 | yes | yes | yes | C10h | {C5^1[2]} |
| C10h > C5h | yes | yes | yes | C10h | {S5^-2[11]} |
| C10h > S10 | yes | yes | yes | C10h | {C5^1[2], S10^-3[12]} |
| C10h > S2 | yes | yes | yes | C10h | {i[10]} |

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

| chain | ordinary | projective | both | abelian from | subgroups |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C12h > C12 | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {C12^1[1]} |
| C12h > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {sigma_h[18]} |
| C12h > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {C2[6]} |
| C12h > C2h | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {C2[6], sigma_h[18]} |
| C12h > C3 | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {C3^1[4]} |
| C12h > C3h | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {S3^-1[14]} |
| C12h > C4 | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {C4^1[3]} |
| C12h > C4h | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {C4^1[3], S4^-1[15]} |
| C12h > C6 | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {C6^1[2]} |
| C12h > C6h | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {C6^1[2], S3^-1[14]} |
| C12h > S12 | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {S12^-5[13]} |
| C12h > S2 | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {i[12]} |
| C12h > S4 | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {S4^-1[15]} |
| C12h > S6 | yes | yes | yes | C12h | {C3^1[4], S6^-1[16]} |

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

| chain | ordinary | projective | both | abelian from | subgroups |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| D11d > C11 | yes | yes | yes | C11 | {C11^1[1]} |
| D11d > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C1h | {sigma_d(1)[33]} |
| D11d > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C2 | {C2'(1)[11]} |
| D11d > C2h | yes | yes | yes | C2h | {C2'(1)[11], sigma_d(1)[33]} |
| D11d > S22 | yes | yes | yes | S22 | {C11^1[1], S22^-9[23]} |
| D11d > C11v > C11 | yes | yes | yes | C11 | {C11^1[1], sigma_d(1)[33]} > {C11^1[1]} |
| D11d > C11v > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C1h | {C11^1[1], sigma_d(1)[33]} > {sigma_d(1)[33]} |
| D11d > D11 > C11 | yes | yes | yes | C11 | {C11^1[1], C2'(1)[11]} > {C11^1[1]} |
| D11d > D11 > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C2 | {C11^1[1], C2'(1)[11]} > {C2'(1)[11]} |

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

| chain | ordinary | projective | both | abelian from | subgroups |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| D5h > C1h | no | yes | no | C1h | {sigma_h[12]} |
| D5h > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C1h | {sigma_v(4)[15]} |
| D5h > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C2 | {C2'(1)[5]} |
| D5h > C5 | yes | no | no | C5 | {C5^1[1]} |
| D5h > C5h | yes | yes | yes | C5h | {S5^-2[10]} |
| D5h > C2v > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C2v | {C2'(1)[5], sigma_h[12]} > {sigma_h[12]} |
| D5h > C2v > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C2v | {C2'(1)[5], sigma_h[12]} > {sigma_v(1)[17]} |
| D5h > C2v > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C2v | {C2'(1)[5], sigma_h[12]} > {C2'(1)[5]} |
| D5h > C5v > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C1h | {C5^1[1], sigma_v(4)[15]} > {sigma_v(4)[15]} |
| D5h > C5v > C5 | yes | yes | yes | C5 | {C5^1[1], sigma_v(4)[15]} > {C5^1[1]} |
| D5h > D5 > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C2 | {C5^1[1], C2'(1)[5]} > {C2'(1)[5]} |
| D5h > D5 > C5 | yes | yes | yes | C5 | {C5^1[1], C2'(1)[5]} > {C5^1[1]} |

Chains whose symbol string names more than one conjugacy class of chains:
- D5h > C1h: 2 classes (ordinary=False, projective=True; ordinary=True, projective=True)
- D5h > C2v > C1h: 2 classes (ordinary=True, projective=True)

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

| chain | ordinary | projective | both | abelian from | subgroups |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| D11h > C11 | yes | no | no | C11 | {C11^1[1]} |
| D11h > C11h | yes | yes | yes | C11h | {S11^-5[22]} |
| D11h > C1h | no | yes | no | C1h | {sigma_h[27]} |
| D11h > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C1h | {sigma_v(7)[33]} |
| D11h > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C2 | {C2'(1)[11]} |
| D11h > C11v > C11 | yes | yes | yes | C11 | {C11^1[1], sigma_v(7)[33]} > {C11^1[1]} |
| D11h > C11v > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C1h | {C11^1[1], sigma_v(7)[33]} > {sigma_v(7)[33]} |
| D11h > C2v > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C2v | {C2'(1)[11], sigma_h[27]} > {sigma_h[27]} |
| D11h > C2v > C1h | yes | yes | yes | C2v | {C2'(1)[11], sigma_h[27]} > {sigma_v(1)[38]} |
| D11h > C2v > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C2v | {C2'(1)[11], sigma_h[27]} > {C2'(1)[11]} |

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

| chain | ordinary | projective | both | abelian from | subgroups |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| O > C3 | yes | no | no | C3 | {C3^1(1)[10]} |
| O > C4 | yes | yes | yes | C4 | {C4^1(1)[18]} |
| O > D2 > C2 | yes | no | no | D2 | {C2(1)[1], C2'(5)[8]} > {C2(1)[1]} |
| O > D2 > C2 | yes | no | no | D2 | {C2(1)[1], C2'(5)[8]} > {C2'(5)[8]} |

| O > D3 > C3 | yes | yes | yes | C3 | {C3^1(1)[10], C2'(2)[5]} > {C3^1(1)[10]} |
| O > D4 > C2 | no | yes | no | C2 | {C4^1(1)[18], C2(2)[2]} > {C2(1)[1]} |
| O > D4 > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C2 | {C4^1(1)[18], C2(2)[2]} > {C2(2)[2]} |

Chains whose symbol string names more than one conjugacy class of chains:
- O > D2 > C2: 2 classes (ordinary=True, projective=False)
- O > D4 > C2: 3 classes (ordinary=False, projective=True; ordinary=True, projective=True)
- O > D4 > D2 > C2: 4 classes (ordinary=True, projective=True)

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.

| chain | ordinary | projective | both | abelian from | subgroups |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| I > C5 | yes | no | no | C5 | {C5^1(1)[36]} |
| I > D5 > C2 | yes | yes | yes | C2 | {C5^1(1)[36], C2(7)[7]} > {C2(7)[7]} |
| I > D5 > C5 | yes | yes | yes | C5 | {C5^1(1)[36], C2(7)[7]} > {C5^1(1)[36]} |
| I > T > C2 | no | yes | no | C2 | {C3^1(1)[16], C3^1(5)[22]} > {C2(4)[4]} |
| I > T > C3 | yes | yes | yes | C3 | {C3^1(1)[16], C3^1(5)[22]} > {C3^1(1)[16]} |
| I > T > D2 > C2 | yes | yes | yes | D2 | {C3^1(1)[16], C3^1(5)[22]} > {C2(4)[4], C2(14)[14]} > {C2(4)[4]} |

### Chains

Chains down to a group with a cyclic rotation group through steps that are multiplicity-free for at least one kind of irrep, one per conjugacy class of chains (simultaneous conjugation in G). `abelian from` is the first group of the chain whose ordinary irreps are all one-dimensional. The last column names every subgroup of the chain by the canonical elements of G that generate it.
